"""Pure selected H+guard extraction, fresh CPU fixture, truthful private-copy test.

No App, policy, SDK, GPU resource query or old checkpoint/bank content access.

from pathlib import Path
import ast,copy,hashlib,json,os,time,traceback
WORK='evaluate_E3_adapted97_source210_initial24_original_queries_once_v1.py'
FULL='e3_full30_existing_limit_observer_v7.py'

def H(p):return hashlib.sha256(Path(p).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
def select_guard(J):
    J=Path(J);raw=(J/WORK).read_bytes();tree=ast.parse(raw)
    manifest=json.loads((J/'E3_SOURCE210_COMPLETE_INPUT_MANIFEST_V1.json').read_bytes());rows={r['relative']:r for r in manifest['files']}
    assert H(J/WORK)==rows[WORK]['SHA'] and H(J/FULL)==rows[FULL]['SHA']
    selected=[n for n in tree.body if isinstance(n,ast.FunctionDef) and n.name in ('H','guard') or isinstance(n,ast.Assign) and any(isinstance(x,ast.Name) and x.id=='MODEL_SHA' for x in n.targets)]
    guard=next(n for n in selected if isinstance(n,ast.FunctionDef) and n.name=='guard')
    assert [x.arg for x in guard.args.args]==['task','job'] and len(guard.args.defaults)==0
    main=next(n for n in tree.body if isinstance(n,ast.FunctionDef) and n.name=='main')
    calls=[n for n in ast.walk(main) if isinstance(n,ast.Call) and isinstance(n.func,ast.Name) and n.func.id=='guard']
    assert len(calls)==1 and ast.unparse(calls[0])=='guard(task, job)'
    scope={'hashlib':hashlib,'Path':Path}
    exec(compile(ast.Module(body=selected,type_ignores=[]),str(J/WORK),'exec'),scope)
    return scope['guard'],dict(worker_SHA=H(J/WORK),component_SHA=H(J/FULL),guard_AST_SHA=hashlib.sha256(ast.dump(guard,include_attributes=False).encode()).hexdigest(),selected_only_H_guard_MODEL_SHA=True,no_worker_import_or_main_execution=True)

    """Actual selected pure function, real postcopy small files, SYNTHETIC Task."""
